eṇpatu viḻukkāṭu taṭavikku mēl varum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 67 maṭṭumēyākum. (pātikku mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟiyiṭukaḷ ovvoṉṟum pattu taṭavikkum kuṟivākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum 112 ovvoṉṟum oru taṭaviyē varukiṟatu). āka, inta 67 kuṟiyīṭukaḷukkum mutaṉmi tantu vācikka muṟpaṭuvatu nallatu eṉkiṟār makātēvaṉ. mottam cumār 400 kuṟiyīṭukaḷil aṭippaṭik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 20 eṉpatu parpōlā karuttu.

7. immuttirip paṭaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟi mārṣal “illasṭirēṭaṭ laṇṭaṉ niyūs” 20.9. 1924 itaḻil veḷiyiṭṭār. ac cañciki 4.10.24 itaḻil ci.ē.kāṭ, ciṭṉi smit ākiyōr cintu veḷi - cumēriya nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ toṭarpu paṟṟik kaṭṭuri veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. cumēriyarkaḷ āriyarkaḷē eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa karuttuṭiya el.ē.vāṭel cintuveḷi makkaḷum āriyarkaḷ eṉat tavaṟāka uṉṉittu, immuttiri vācakaṅkaḷil uḷḷavi vēta, itikāca kālat teyvaṅkaḷ, riṣikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ eṉṟu 1925 l nūl veḷiyiṭṭār. 1931m āṇṭu veḷiyāṉa mārṣal āyvu nūlil cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyaruṭiyatu aṉṟu eṉa mārṣal niṟuviyirunta pōtilum, eḻuttu paṟṟi annūlil oru iyal eḻutiya lāṅṭaṉ, ivveḻuttukkaḷ intō āriya moḻi eṉṟu takka ātāramiṉṟik kuṟippiṭṭār. (acōka pirami eḻuttukaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiyavi; cintu veḷik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ōracic coṟkaḷ; eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ēṟkattakka karuttukkaḷiyum lāṅṭaṉ kūṟiyiruntār). pirāṉ nāt pōṉṟa piṟarum piṉṉar avvaḻiyē ceṉṟu iḻukkiṉar. ivviyaliṉ iṟutippakutiyik kāṇka.

8. pacipik peruṅkaṭal naṭuvēyuḷḷa īsṭar tīvil cila nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar kiṭitta marappalakikaḷil cetukkap paṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷukkum cintu eḻuttukkaḷukkum uṟavu uṇṭu eṉṟu ṭi heveci 1933-l oru kaṭṭuri veyiyiṭṭār. ik karuttu tavaṟāṉatu eṉa iṉṟu aṉivarum otukkiviṭṭa karuttu.

9. iṉṟum intiya nākarikattiṉ perumpakutikku aṭippaṭiyāka uḷḷa (tamiḻiya) cintu nākarikap paṇpāṭṭuṭaṉ ciṟu aḷavukku intō-āriya moḻiyiṉar paṇpāṭum uṭaṉ kalanta kālamākiya ki.mu.2000-1500; maṟṟum ataṟkup piṉṉar. caṅka kālattamiḻakattuṭaṉ vaṭamēṟku, vaṭintiyap pakutikaḷuṭaṉ irunta toṭarpu paṟṟi iyal 8l kaṇṭōm. ataṟkum vekumuntiya kālattilum (ki.mu.3000-2000 allatu ataṟkum muṉṉar) cintup pakutikkum/ tamiḻakattukkum mika neruṅkiya toṭarpu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum attakiya toṭarpukaḷik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ, caṅka ilakkiyattilum uḷḷaṉa eṉpatiyum ār. pālakiruṣṇaṉ ‘ūr maṟṟum iṭappeyar āyviṉ’ mūlam kaṇṭuḷḷār. Journal of Tamil Studies cūṉ 2010 itaḻil “tamiḻccintu nākarikam? vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil koṟki, vañci, toṇṭi mutaliya iṭap peyarkaḷ; caṅka ilakkiyattil oṭṭakam elumpi uṇṇuvatu paṟṟiya kuṟippu” eṉṟa ciṟanta āyvil terivittuḷḷār. avar cuṭṭum iṭappeyar mutaliya oppumikaḷ cila varumāṟu:

(i) cintu nākarikappakutiyil (iṉṟu pākistāṉ - āpkāṉistāṉ) uḷḷa tamiḻiya iṭappeyarkaḷ; koṟki (Gorkay, Gorki, Gorkhi), vañci, toṇṭi, maturi (Matri), uṟi (uri), kūṭal (Kudalgarh), kōḻi (Koli); kaḷḷūr (“perum peyarkkaḷḷūr” akanāṉūṟu 256) intiyat tuṇikkaṇṭattil mottam 38 kaḷḷūr / kallūrkaḷ uḷḷaṉavām.

(ii) mali, kōṭu, kuṉṟu, vari, muṭi, cuṉi, ēṉal, kāṭu, kāṉal, puli, puri, aṭavi, ā / yāṟu, kaḻaṉi, maṇal, nilam, nili, tari, teru, maṉi, tiri, kaḷari, pāli, curam, kuṟumpi pōṉṟa oṟṟic col ūrppeyar (ovvoṉṟum caṟṟē tirinta vaṭivil) aṅku ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa.

(iii) ulakeṅkum paḻaṅkuṭippeyarkaḷ maṉṉarkuṭip peyarkaḷ, kuṟunilattalivar kuṭip peyarkaḷ ākiyavi) perumaḷavukku ūrppeyarkaḷil iruntē peṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉvarum tamiḻ nāṭṭup peyarkaḷiṉ caṟṟē tirinta vaṭivaṅkaḷ cintup pakutiyil ūrp peyarkaḷāka iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa (aṇṭar, āyar, kaḷamar, kāḷi, koṅkar, tuṭiyaṉ, pāṇaṉ, maṟavar, aṟivālā, utiyaṉ, kaḷaṅkāy, karikāl (Garigal) kiḷḷi, kōti, celiyaṉvālā, cēraṉ, cēraṉvāli, cōḷā, pāṇṭiyar, pāṇṭiyaṉ vālā, poṟi, vaḻuti, atiyamāṉ, aṉṉi, ātaṉ, utiyaṉ, kaṭṭi, kōṭaṉ, cāttaṉ, titiyaṉ, tittaṉ, paṉṉi, naḷḷi, neṭumāṉ, kōṭaṉ, pāri, piṭṭaṉ, piṇṭaṉ, pēkaṉ, matti, miñili, mūvaṉ, veḷiyaṉ.

(iv) aintiṇikaḷil oṉṟākap pāliyic caṅka ilakkiyam kuṟippiṭiṉum uṇmiyāṉa pāli desert teṉṉāṭṭil illi. cintu nākarikam tār pālivaṉatti oṭṭiya kujarāt pakutiyilum paraviyiruntati akaḻvāyvukaḷē niṟuviyuḷḷaṉa. antappāli potumakkaḷ niṉivil nilittu iruntatāl tāṉ aintiṇiyil pāliyiyum cērttaṉar; atu maṭṭumalla, pāli vilaṅkāṉa oṭṭakam cārnta oru viyakkattakka uṇmiyāṉa kuṟippum akanāṉūṟu 245 il ‘tatar veḷ eṉpu kaṭuṅkāl oṭṭakattu alku paci tīrkkum’ (vēṟu uṇavu etuvum kiṭṭāta niliyil pacitta elumpiyum tiṉṉum eṉpatu poruḷ. oṭṭakam elumpu tiṉpatu eṉpatu vilaṅkiyalāḷar ēṟṟuḷḷatākum) icceyti ēṭṭil eḻutātu potumakkaḷ niṉivilum cevivaḻikkati / pāṭalkaḷilum neṭuṅkālam iruntu piṉṉarc caṅkappāṭalilum ēṟiyirukkumeṉpar pālakiruṣṇaṉ. (iṅku “tamiḻar varalāṟu, ki.pi.600 vari (1929) nūlil pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār iyal 5 iṉ 19m pattiyil kuṟittuḷḷa piṉvarum karuttiyum niṉivu kūrka.

 “vētattil varum “pañca janāh” eṉpati paṇṭiyōr, nam kālattavar, yārālum pontikiyāka viḷakka muṭiyavilli. paṇṭu vaṭa intiya makkaḷum aintiṇi iṉakkuḻukkaḷākap pirintiruntati iccol niṉivūṭṭukiṟatu. eṉavē āriyac caṭaṅku tōṉṟuvataṟku muṟpaṭṭu vaṭa intiyāvil irunta aintiṇi iṉakkuḻukkaḷi atu kuṟittiruklām eṉṟu uṉṉikkiṟēṉ. āyiṉum tamiḻ ilakkiya aṟivaṟṟavarkaḷum “āriyattukku muntiya intiyāvukkum, āriya intiyāvukkum iṭiyīṭaṟṟa varalāṟṟut toṭarpu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati uṇara maṟuppavarkaḷum eṉatu karutti ēṟpatilli”.

(v) pālakiruṣṇaṉuṭiya mukkiyamāṉa muṭivukaḷ varumāṟu.

(1) cintu nākarika moḻi mūlatirāviṭam Proto Dravidan eṉpataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ valuvāṉavi.

(2) ūrppeyar āyvukaḷ tarum oḷiyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl cintu nākarikat toṭarcciyik kaṇṭaṟiyum āyvukaḷum, akaḻvāyvukkāṉa iṭat terivukaḷum mēlum payaṉuḷḷaṉavāka amiyum.

(3) oru pāl cintu nākarikattiṉ paḻantamiḻt toṭarpi niṟuvum muyaṟcikaḷum maṟupāl caṅka kālattiṟku muntiya pulappeyarvu maṟṟum paḻantamiḻt toṉma marapu āyvu muyaṟcikaḷum orē nāṇayattiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷ tām. oṉṟaṉ nalivukaḷi nīkki, valivukaḷi mēmpaṭuttuvatu maṟṟatu ākum. (ākavē iruvaki ayvukaḷum tēvi)

10. cintu veḷi muttirikaḷ 750 ai āḻntu ārāyntu 1934l nūl veḷiyiṭṭavar ji.ār.haṇṭar. ivar tāṉ mutaṉ mutalil avaṟṟiṉ moḻi intō āriyam alla; ōracic col mikunta (tirāviṭa moḻi pōṉṟa) moḻiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu niṟuviyavar. cintuveḷi eḻuttē piramiyiṉ tāy eṉṟa lāṅṭaṉ karuttu ivarukku uṭaṉpāṭē. cātāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷukku cintuveḷi makkaḷ ōlikaḷ pōṉṟa aḻiyum poruṭkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyiruppar eṉkiṟār.

11. cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭaruṭiyatu eṉṟa mārṣal muṭivi valuppaṭuttiyum, annākarikamoḻi eḻuttu tol 140 toltamiḻiyac cintu nākarikam tirāviṭa moḻi eḻuttē eṉpatiyum nērmiyāṉa aṟiñar evarum maṟukkavoṇṇā vakiyil pala āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷi iṭiviṭātu veḷiyiṭṭavar es. hīrās pātiriyār. avar 1953l veḷiyiṭṭa ciṟanta nūl; ‘toṉmi intō - naṇṇilak kari nākarika āyvu’ eṉpatākum. ivar cintu moḻiyi muntu (purōṭō) tirāviṭa moḻiyākap paṭittuḷḷār. ivarukku muṉ ivvāyvil īṭupaṭṭavarkaḷip pōla ivarum valamiruntu iṭamākavē iv veḻuttukkaḷip paṭikkiṟār. ovvoru kuṟiyīṭum aciyiyō mey/uyir eḻuttiyō kuṟippatalla; ovvoru kuṟiyīṭum oru col (atāvatu paṭaveḻuttu / colluruvaṉ; Logographic); vācakaṅkaḷ iyaṟpeyar alla; avi kūṟṟukkaḷum viḷakkaṅkaḷum ākum; iviyē avar aṭippaṭik kōṭpāṭukaḷ. mikap perum eṇṇikkiyil varum eṉṉum kuṟi, “māṟaṉatu”, ‘vēlaṉatu’ eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl uṭimip poruḷil varum “atu” eṉṉum
collik kuṟippatu; cilaviṭaṅkaḷil cuṭṭup poruḷilum varum; eṉṟār.

12. paṇṭiya eḻuttukkaḷil “olikoḷ vaṭivam” (Phonograph) allatu rīpas Rebus eṉpatil aṭaṅkuvaṉa:

a) eṉṟu mīṉpaṭam pōṭṭu “mīṉavaṉi” (aracaṉi) kuṟippiṭutal

ā) ōviya eḻuttup putir (Rebus / homophony); e.kā 

bee 	leaf 	= 	belief 
(WATER) CAN 	die date = Candidate

13. pala cintuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷi ik kōṭpāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭiyil hīrās paṭittuḷḷār. avar paṭittuḷḷa cila vācakaṅkaḷ:-

āṇ atu mīṉ kuṭa vāḻkki naṇṭ āṇ
“pacupati” muttiri

āṇnaṇṭ vāl key kuṭamīṉ atu āṇ
“The Lord of the Water - jar and the Fish is the weakening and strengthening of the Lord”

kaṭavuḷ eṭ ōriṭa mīṉ pēr taḷi atu

atu taḷipēr mīṉ ōriṭa eṭ kaṭavuḷ


This is the (eight) formed God one of whose sides (forms) (is) the sprinkled great fish.
atu ari valil mīṉ

mīṉ valil ari atu

This is the weak toddy of the Minas

cintu veḷi eḻuttukku tol tirāviṭam eṉa hīrās mutaṟkaṇ tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuviyati iṉṟu nērmiyāṉa aṟiñar aṉivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum immuttirikaḷ perumpālum etaṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpatik karutuma poḻutu hīrās paṭittuḷḷa colluruvaṉ logographic vācakaṅkaḷ perumpālum eṟkat takkaviyāka illi.

cumēriya eḻuttu muṟi cintu eḻuttu muṟiyiliruntutāṉ uruvāṉatu eṉpatu hīrās koḷki (1953m āṇṭu nūl, pak 249-278). cīṉa eḻuttu muṟiyum ōraḷavukku avvāṟē eṉkiṟār avar.

14. (i) hīrācukkuppiṉ cintup poṟippukaḷit tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻiyākappaṭitta cilariṉ vācakaṅkaḷit tantuviṭṭup piṉṉar, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ, mativāṇaṉ jīvā mutaliyavar āyvukaḷ caṟṟu virivākat tarappaṭum.

(ii) pērcarvis pōṉṟa cilar vācippukaḷiṉ mātirikaḷ varumāṟu:

vālṭar pērcarvīs (1983) ōviya eḻuttup putir (rīpas) kōṭpāṭṭiṉ paṭi vācittuḷḷār.



e.kā: 		mutalippaṭam 	= 	mutali Chief
= 	noṟukku / nūṟu
nel payir paṭam 			= 	nelā (nilā Moon)

ivar paṭittuḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷ cila varumāṟu:

āṉ paṭukāru ārapiriki ā ampara paṭā cūr
“cūr paṭāmparā ārapiriki paṭu kāraṉ”
“Patukaran powerful (noble) chief of the surrounding settlements”

āṉ pir (ca)āy = cāy piṟiyāḷaṉ

cuppiramaṇiyam maliyāṇṭi (1978) : ivar tolkāppiyac centamiḻāka muttiri vācakaṅkaḷip paṭittuḷḷār.

kiḷiṭ akamatu viṉṭars (1985 mutal): ivar colluruvaṉ logographic eḻuttukkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷār.

ta cey u-i miṉ-i lu aṇṇal
aṇṇal lu miṉ-i-u-i ceyta
Much righteoumess, let it shine, bring here virtue.

us pukaḷ, miṉ uss kāvēi
kāvēi uss miṉpukaḷ us
Balance blooms Gods justice - a shining glorious fate
peṉāṉ spikṉiyū cālek (pōlantu)

li maa we munnu wuri ka (kaa uuri mun wel maal)
= protection end of the world before white great man)

Ya mii mii Yiru
Ai mil mil Iru = Iru mii mii ai great heavenly heavenly king

15. cintuppoṟippukaḷ tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻi cārntavitām eṉpatu paṟṟip paltuṟi, paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷiṭam oru mitta karuttu uḷḷatu – ittuṟiyil nāṟpatāṇṭu nuṭpamākavum kaṭiṉamākavum uḻitta piṉlāntu aṟiñar askō parpōlā, nam tamiḻ nāṭṭu aṟiñar airāvatam makātēvaṉ uṭpaṭa. (tirāviṭa moḻic cārpuṭiyatākap palarum paṭittuḷḷa pōtilum yāruṭiya vācippum iṉṟu vari aṟiñar ulakil ēṟkappaṭavilli. īrās, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ, nāracāv, pērcarvīs, viṉṭars, cālek, mativāṇaṉ, pūrṇacantira jīvā ākiya yāruṭiya vācippumē muḻumiyāka ēṟkappaṭavilli). cintu eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻiya moḻi cārntavi eṉpatu maṭṭum ulakaḷavil nallaṟiñar kaḷāl ēṟkappaṭṭuḷḷatu - kāṇka:-

sṭāṉli vālparṭ (1991): intiyāvukku ōr aṟimukam ‘pala vakiyāṉa ātāraṅkaḷiliruntum nām karutuvatu avarkaḷ tol tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻukkup pāṭṭaṉ muṟiyākak kūṭiya oru moḻi avarkaḷ moḻiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉpatum ākum.”

“We assume from various shreds of evidence that they were proto Dravidian, possibly using a language that was a grandfather of modern Tamil” (Stanley Wolpert : An Introduction to India, University of California Press 1991)

jē.em.rāparṭs (2010) : pelikaṉ ulaka varalāṟu “ teṉṉintiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkivarum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu iyipuṭiya oru moḻiyic cārntaviyāka (cintu muttiric coṟkaḷ) irukkalāmeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

It now seems at least likely that they are part of a language akin to the Dravidian tongues still used inSouthern India (J.M.Roberts History of the World, Pelican 1992)

kamil cuvelapil (1990) tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal – ōr aṟimukam “cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ moḻi eṉṉa eṉṟu iṟutiyāka niṟuvappaṭumpoḻutu atu tirāviṭamoḻi cārntatāka amivataṟkē vāyppumika atikam.” (“The most probable candidate is and remins some form of Dravidian” Dravidian Linguistics An Introduction. Pondicherry 1990: Chap VI: Dravidian and Harappan) 

em.pi.emeṉō cintuppoṟippukaḷi vācittu aṟivataṟkāṉa taṭayaṅkaḷi vēṟu emmoḻik kuṭumpattiyum viṭa tirāviṭa moḻikkuṭumpattil tāṉ kāṇa vāyppu atikam uṇṭu. (The most promising language family in which to look for clues
that might aid in their tranlation has seemed to be Dravidian” (ENCYCLOPAEDIA AMERICANA; 1999)

askō parpōlā

16. 1960 kaḷiliruntu cintu eḻuttu āyviyē tamatu mukkiyamāṉa vāḻnāṭ paṇiyāka koṇṭuḷḷavar inta piṉlāntu nāṭṭaṟiñar. ittuṟiyil avar paṭittuḷḷa iṉṟiyamiyāta nūlkaḷi mēlē iraṇṭām pattiyiṟ kaṇṭōm. tamatu āyvu muṭivukaḷi 2010 cūṉ 25 aṉṟu kōyamputtūril ulakat tamiḻc cemmoḻi mānāṭṭil āṟṟiya cintu eḻuttuc cikkal tirāviṭat [tirāviṭa moḻit] tīrvu eṉṉum poḻivil curukkamākat tantuḷḷār. mukkiyamāṉa cilavaṟṟik kāṇpōm.

17. tol intō - airōppiya moḻi pēcunar ki.mu. 3000kkup piṉṉar teṉ - kiḻakku airōppiyap pakutiyiliruntu (karuṅkaṭal - kāspiyaṉ kaṭalkaḷukku vaṭakkiluḷḷa pakuti) mēṟku, teṟku, teṉ kiḻakkākap paravat toṭaṅkiya poḻutu ki.mu.2300 - 1500 kālam cārnta pākṭiriyā – mārjiyāṉā tolliyal nākarikat (Bactria and Margiana Archeological Complex = BMAC)  tolliyal eccappakutiyit tāṇṭi ki.mu.1900 – 1600 il īrāṉ - cintup pakutikku varalāyiṉar. (ki.mu 1900 – 1600 eṉpatu hārappā nākarikattiṉ kaṭip pakutiyākum). varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyal cāṉṟukaḷ aṭippaṭiyil (virivāṉa vivaraṅkaḷ muntiya iyaliṉ kaṭicip pakutiyil) – kuṟippāka rikvēta ttilēyē kāṇappaṭum tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal, ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ, tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil, hārappā nākarika makkaḷ pēciyatu tirāviṭa moḻiyākattāṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum. (Historical Linguistics, thus suggests that the Harappans probably spoke a Dravidian language)

18. cintu eḻutti vācikka muyalvōr palaruṭiya piṉvarum karutukōḷkaḷ tavaṟu eṉpar parpōlā:-

(i) cintu lipiyil uḷḷa vaṭivam pōṉṟa oṉṟu vēṟu enta lipiyilum iruntāl antap piṟa lipiyiṉ oli / poruḷtāṉ cintu vaṭivattiṟkum eṉṟu tavaṟākak karutik koḷvatu.

(ii) cemittiya “meyyeḻuttaṭippaṭi vaṇṇamāli” Semitic Consonantal alphablet ai aṭiyoṟṟi uruvāṉatu piramilipiyākum. cintu lipiyiliruntu uruvāṉatalla piramilipi; eṉiṉum cintulipiyiliruntu pirami uruvāṉatākat tavaṟāka eṇṇik koṇṭu pirami aṭippaṭiyil cintup poṟippukaḷip paṭikka muyalum tavaṟāṉa pōkku.

19. ulakil enta aciyeḻuttu / vaṇṇamāli varivaṭivamum (Syllabic or alphabetic script) tōṉṟuvataṟkum muṉṉar tōṉṟiya cintu eḻuttu collaciyaṉ varivaṭiva mākattāṉ logo - syllabic writing system) irukka vēṇṭum. avi perumpālum mēlē viḷakkiya (oli oppumi homophone aṭippaṭiyil uruvākiya) rīpas vakic collaciyaṉ Logosyllabic varivaṭivaṅkaḷē, tamiḻil “mīṉ” eṉpatu fish  aiyum kuṟittatu eṉka. cintu muttirikaḷil “10 kuṟikaḷil oṉṟu mīṉ’ eṉṟa aḷavukku mīṉ uruvam (veṟum mīṉ uruvam, maṟṟum arttam māṟuvatik kuṟikkum palvēṟu aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷik diacritical marks koṇṭa uruvaṅkaḷ) kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

20. tamiḻ vaṭam / vaṭamaram (=ālamaram) eṉpatil varum ‘vaṭa’ kayiṟiyum kuṟittatu. (kayiṟu pōṉṟatu ālamara viḻutu, eṉavē) vaṭaticiyiyum kuṟittatu, “vaṭa mīṉ’ vaṭaticiyil uḷḷa turuva naṭcattirattik kuṟittatu. pōṉṟa ālamarat tōṟṟam koṇṭa cintu vaṭivaṅkaḷ oli oppumi aṭippaṭiyil ‘vaṭa’ eṉpatik kuṟittaṉa. tamiḻ ‘vaṭa’ eṉpataṟku ivvāṟu iraṇṭu arttam uḷḷattit tavaṟākappurintu koṇṭu rikvētam  (1, 24.7 atu ki.mu.1000 cārntatu ākalām) vāṉattil varuṇaṉ tāṅkum ālamaram oṉṟiṉ vērkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. harappā muttiri 179l ālamarattukkuḷ kāṇum maṉita urutteyvam inta marattik kuṟippatākalām.

21. vētakkati iṉṉoṉṟu, turuva naṭcattiramāṉatu ēṉi viṇmīṉkaḷōṭu kāṟṟuk kayiṟukaḷāl ropes of wind piṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatākak kūṟukiṟatu. inta aṭippaṭiyil piṉvarum vācakaṅkaḷi parpōlā tarukiṟār.
hārappā H-9:  I	III= eḻumīṉ (captariṣi = Ursa mayor viṇmīṉ kūṭṭam

mokeñ 	M.414 vaṭamīṉ = turuva naṭcattiram

22. tirāviṭa muri (-v-, -nt -) [=to bend, to be bent] muruvu / muruku [=kammal, vaḷiyal kaṭakam aḻaku] murukaṉ. eṉavē vaḷiyattic cintu eḻuttil iraṇṭu vaṭṭaṅkaḷ kuṟittaṉa. ēṉ iraṇṭu vaṭṭaṅkaḷ? oru vaṭṭam teḷivāṉa poruḷ tarātu; mēlum kammal, vaḷiyal ākiyavaṟṟi iṇiyākat tāṉē aṇikiṉṟaṉar? tamiḻnāṭṭilum ēṉ intiyā veṅkum (nalamāṉa kuḻantip pēṟukkāka) cūluṟṟa tāykku vaḷikāppu aṇital; puṉitamaraṅkaḷ, teyvaṅkaḷukku vaḷiyal aṇivittal
ākiya caṭaṅkukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ippiṉṉaṇiyil

mo 112 il kāṇum	III 	mo 241 il

aṟumīṉ (kiruttikā) 		= kāṇum vēḷ muruka

(murukaṉ)(kārttikēyaṉ) 		atē arttam

eṉṟu kūṟalām.

ninatōvāri muttiri Nd 1 l (mutukil aintuvari koṇṭa) aṇiṟpiḷḷi oṉṟik kāṇalām. atē pōṉṟa aṇil marakkiḷiyil talikīḻākat toṅkik koṇṭiruppati nuṣārō muttiri, mo 1202, hā 771 ākiyavaṟṟilum kāṇalām. avaṟṟil kāṇum iraṇṭiyum murukappiḷḷi eṉappaṭikkalām. yāḻppāṉit tamiḻ āṭavar peyarkaḷil itupōṉṟu piḷḷi eṉṟu muṭiyum peyarkaḷ pala.

11 	eṉpati murukavēḷ eṉappaṭittōm. atil II eṉpatu vēḷ tāṉ eṉa niṟuviṭa iṉṉoru cāṉṟik kāṇalām:

vēḷ muruka 		mīṉ veḷ = veḷḷik kōḷ
(hā 723) 		hā 669

veḷ white;  ataṉuṭiya oliyoppumic col homophone tāṉ vēḷ. ivviraṇṭukkum iṉṉoru oliyoppumic col veḷi (=open public, space (in general), intervening space [tēva rulakattukkum pūmikkum iṭippaṭṭa (camaskiruta antarīkṣam), uḻavil irucālkaḷukkum iṭippaṭṭa pakuti] eṉpatākum. vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil varum III kōṭukaḷ kuṟiyīṭu mūvulakattiyum kuṟippatākak karuta iṭamuṇṭu. tamiḻ “veḷḷi” veḷḷik kōḷi maṭṭumaṉṟi viṇ mīṉiyum potuvākak kuṟikkiṟatu. (a.citamparanātaṉ ceṭṭiyārāṅkila - tamiḻ akarāti 1965: star = viṇmīṉ; vāṉveḷḷi) veḷḷiyi viṭiveḷḷi, viṭimīṉ eṉṟum aḻippatuṇṭu.

23. ulakat tamiḻc cemmoḻi mānāṭṭu uriyil (25-6-2010) parpōlā iṟutiyākak kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:

“oṉṟukkoṉṟu cariyākap poruntum rīpas muṟi vācippukaḷik kaṇicamāṉa eṇṇikkiyil kaṇṭōm. avaṟṟukku aṭittaḷamāka amintu muḻu ātaravutarum moḻiyiyal ceytikaḷiyum paṇpāṭṭuta taṉmikaḷiyum viḷakki immuṭivukaḷ taṟceyalāṉa pōli oppumikaḷ allaveṉpatiyum niṟuviyuḷḷōm. aṟiviyal rītiyāṉa karāṟāṉa āyvu muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi vācittuḷḷōm - māntaṉ moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttu varivaṭivat (lipi) tōṟṟam vaḷarcci varalāṟu, puriyāta lipiyip paṭittaṟiyak kiyāḷa vēṇṭiya aṟivupūrvamāṉa muṟi, ivaṟṟiyellām karuttiṟ koṇṭum, varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyaliṉ (tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ oppu nōkku moḻiyiyal uṭpaṭa) āyvuc ceytikaḷiṉ piṉpulattilum ivvāyvu naṭantuḷḷatu. muttirikaḷiṉ paṭa uruvaṅkaḷ etik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉpatiyum mikak kavaṉamākavē karutip pārttuḷḷōm. paṇṭi intiyap paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟu harappā nākarikattiṉ (tōṟṟam, taṉmi, vaḷarcci, piṟa paṇpāṭukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu mutaliya) cūḻal ākiyavaṟṟi aṭiccaṭṭamākak koṇṭu pārttāl āyvil kūṟappaṭum muṭivukaḷ niyāyamāka ēṟkat takkavi eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. ammuṭivukaḷum muḻumiyāṉa teḷivāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum ātāraṅkaḷum aminta ciṟṟaḷaviṉavāṉa āyvuppulac ceytikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tāṉ (maṟukkoṇāta vakiyil) amintuḷḷaṉa. [appulaṅkaḷ aracamarattōṭu iṇinta makappēṟu vēṇṭutal nampikki; vāṉiyal, kālakkaṇippu ivaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭiya mukkiyamāṉa paḻiya intu matat toṉmakkati; intumatattilum, paḻantamiḻ matattilum mutaṉmipeṟṟa (murukaṉ pōṉṟa) teyvaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa]

mēṟcoṉṉavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil cintu eḻuttip paṭittaṟivataṟkāṉa vāyil tiṟakkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu eṉa uṟutiyāka nāṉ nampukiṟēṉ. cintu muttiri vācakaṅkaḷ toltirāviṭa moḻiyiltāṉ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatit terintu koṇṭōm. anta lipiyiṉ amippum evvaḻikaḷil muyaṉṟāl atip paṭikkalām eṉpatiyum aṟintuḷḷōm. ituvarit tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat terintukoṇṭa ceytikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ virivāṉa cūḻalkaḷum mēṟkoṇṭu ceyya vēṇṭiya āyvukaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa, eṉiṉum cila cikkalkaḷ irukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉa. pala paṭaveḻuttukaḷiṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭa māṉa poruḷ iṉṉum teriyavaravilli. toltirāviṭac coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ marapu vaḻakkukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ namakku muḻumiyākat teriyavilliyāki yāl, poruttamāṉa vācippu muyaṟcikaḷ mūlam mēlum palavaṟṟip paṭippataṟkum appaṭip paṭittu muṉ vikkap paṭuvaṉavaṟṟi naṉku caripārttu aṟivulakam ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vakiyil niṟuvuvataṟkum vāyppukaḷ mikakkuṟivākavē uḷḷaṉa. inta niliyil tamiḻiyum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiyum tāymoḻiyākap pēcupavarkaḷ inta āyvil ūkkattuṭaṉ talippaṭṭu mēlum āyvu ceyya vēṇṭum. (vaṭa amerikkāvil mekcikō nāṭṭil uḷḷa) mayā nākarika eḻuttum col- aciyaṉ (logo-syllabic) varivaṭivattiltāṉ uḷḷatu. mayā moḻiyit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷukku anta lipiyip paṭikkum muṟikaḷil payiṟci aḷitta piṉṉar āyvu vēkamāka vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. cintu muttiri varivaṭivattip poṟuttum ōraḷavukku ituporuntum. palkalikkaḻakap pērāciriyar pōṉṟa vallunar maṭṭumallātu. ārvamuḷḷa potumakkaḷum cintu muttirippaṭa urukkaḷ etik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu kuṟittu cintittup poruttamāṉa karuttukaḷit terivikkalām; ivvāṟu ceyya oruvar tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatilli. eṉiṉum intiyap paṇpāṭu teṉāciya iyaṟkit tiṇip poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟiya tiṟamāṉa pulami iruntāl ittakiya āyvu mēlum ciṟakkum tamiḻ makkaḷ ivvakiyil terivikkum āyvukkaruttukaḷiyellām ceṉṉiyil (taramaṇi miya pāliṭekṉik vaḷākam) rōjā muttiyā āyvu nūlakattil airāvatam makātēvaṉ niṟuviyuḷḷa cintu āyvu miyam oruṅkiṇittu ūkkuvikkalām. veḷippaṭum taramāṉa karuttukaḷi ammiyam taṉatu valittaḷattilum veḷiyiṭalām.

24. ittuṟiyil mēlum muṉṉēṟṟam kāṇa vēṇṭumāyiṉ paṇpāṭṭu āyvu vallunar, tamiḻiya moḻiyiyal vallunar, akaḻvāyvu - paṇṭi nākarika āyvāḷar ākiyōruṭiya oruṅkiṇinta uḻippu tēvi eṉṟa āṇṭru rāpiṉcaṉ 2001 karuttum ituvē: Lost languages: the enigma of the world”s undeciphered scripts McGraw Hill; New york (“a reanalysis of the seals and other inscriptions on the basis of their chronological and spatial distribution .... if combined with a more intensive analysis of the signs and their patterns, the inputs of “cultural’ experts like Parpola and Mahadevan, and the linguistic control provided by professional Dravidianists may in due course yield solid results. Collaboration seems to be the name of the game here, since no one scholar can hope to command sufficient knowledge of ancient writing systems, Indus valley and the relevant languages.

ai. makātēvaṉ

25. parpōlāvi aṭuttu cintu eḻuttu āyvil nāṟpatu aimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka āyvu neṟi piṟaḻāmal muṉippuṭaṉ īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñar ai. makātēvaṉ i.ā.pa. ēḻāṇṭu kaṭumiyāka uḻittu 1977l veḷiyiṭṭa cintu muttiri vācakaṅkaḷiṉ niralpaṭuttiya aṭaṅkaṟpaṭṭiyali piṟṟi āyvāḷar aṉivarum payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. irumoḻi vācakam / coṟpaṭṭiyalkoṇṭa poṟippu etuvum kiṭittā
loḻiya cintu muttiri vācakaṅkaḷi muḻumiyākappaṭittaṟiya iyalātu eṉṉum (parpōlā mutaliya) ittuṟi vallunar palaruṭiya karuttē ivaruṭiyatumām.. cintup poṟippukaḷ “oru collukku oru eḻuttu” “oru acikku oru eḻuttu”ākiya iruvaki varivaṭivamum kalantatāka ivar karutukiṟār. ammuttiri vācakaṅkaḷiṉ moḻi paḻantirāviṭam eṉṟa parpōlā mutaliya nallaṟiñar karuttē makātēvaṉ karuttumākum. piṉvarum aṭippaṭikaḷiṉmēl - atāvatu :-

(a) oru collukku oru eḻuttu āka amiyum cilavaṟṟit teṉṉintiyāvil kiṭṭiyuḷḷa cintu eḻuttukaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷ cā [ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭu cārnta cūlūr taṭṭu; (tiṇṭivaṉam arukil uḷḷa) cāṇūr īmattāḻip poṟippukaḷ; kēraḷa mānilam paṭṭaṇattil (muciṟi?) kiṭṭiya ōṭṭut tuṇṭil uḷḷa muruku kuṟiyīṭu; ki.mu.1500 cārnta cempiyaṉkaṇṭiyūr kaṟkōṭariyil uḷḷa (makātēvaṉ “muruku = aṉ - eṉappaṭittuḷḷa) poṟippu; pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyilum;

(ā) cintuppakuti makkaḷukkum, aṅku ciṟu eṇṇikkiyil intō āriya moḻi pēcunarukkum ki.mu.2500-1500 kāla aḷavil nikaḻnta palatuṟik kalappup paṇpāṭṭu eccaṅkaḷ vētamoḻi / camaskiruta nūlkaḷ; caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ; piṟavaralāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟilkāṇappaṭuvatāl avaṟṟiyum nuṇuki ārāynta aṭippaṭiyilum;

makātēvaṉ cintu muttiri vācakaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum paṇpāṭu tamiḻakap paṇpāṭṭi ottiruntatu eṉṟa karutukōḷukku vantu atukuṟittu tamiḻnāṭu varalāṟṟup pēraviyiṉ 16vatu karuttaraṅkil (9-10-2009; tirucci) Vestiges of Indus Civilization in old Tamil eṉṟa āyvuriyi veḷiyiṭṭār. 2010 caṉavariyil avvariya āyvuriyit tamiḻākki “cintuveḷip paṇpāṭum caṅka ilakkiyamum” eṉṟa peyaril cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.

26. mēṟcoṉṉa 9-10-09 āyviliruntum itē aṭippaṭiyil makātēvaṉ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa vēṟucila āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷil iruntum cila ceytikaḷit taruvatu porutta mākum:
 
(i) muruku (murukaṉ) eṉa aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭuḷḷa cintu ṉinaṭipacayaa karuttukkuṟiyīṭu cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūrkaṟ kōṭariyil uḷḷatu.

(ii) poṟu = kāvu (puṟam 206 il varum ‘kāviṉemkalaṉē’ eṉpatil kāvutal cumattal cumattal eṉṉum poruḷil vantatu) ->poṟi -> rikvēta parata Bharata  (oru mukkiyamāṉa kuṭiyiṉar). kāvaṭiyic cuṭṭiya cintu vaṭivam piṉvarumāṟu ciṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu vēta camaskirutac coṟkaḷik kuṟittaṉa eṉalām.

āka peru vīraṉ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa

vaṭivatti makātēvaṉ eṉṟa poruḷ koṇṭa camaskirutac col vaṭivamākak koṇṭaṉar. ammoḻiyil ḷakaratti lakaramākak koṇṭu ‘nīlakaṇṭaṉ’ eṉattavaṟākak karutip pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintu uruvāṉa “(nīla niṟa)nañci uṇṭa kaḻutti uṭiyavaṉ” eṉap purāṇakkatiyum piṉṉarp puṉiyappaṭṭu makātēvaṉ nīlakaṇṭaṉ āṉāṉ. (nīḷ kaṇṭaṉ eṉṟa toltamiḻp peyari maṟantuviṭṭa niliyil) kaṟi / nīla maṇi miṭaṟṟu aṇṇal eṉṟa putiya peyari vaṭamoḻi “nīla kaṇṭaṉiṉ’ coṟpeyarppākakkoṇṭu, appurāṇak katiyiyum ēṟkumnili puṟanāṉūṟṟuk kālattilēyē ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu. teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum nīlakaṇṭaṉ, maṇikaṇṭaṉ, nañcuṇṭaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟu ituvē.


irā. mativāṇaṉ

27. tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻi pēcunar ki.mu.10000 kku muṉṉarē intiyāveṅkum paraviyiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum (tirāviṭar intiyāvi viṭṭu vaṭakkē paraviya) tirāviṭar ēṟṟattiṉ Dravidian Ascent  piṉṉar intō - airōppiyam, urāl
- altāyk mutaliya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ atiliruntu uruvākiyirukkalām eṉpatiyum mutal iyalil kaṇṭōm. ñā.tēvanēyaṉ tamiḻvaralāṟu (1967) il kūṟiyatu varumāṟu.
 
“mokañcotarō muttiriyeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ mūla vaṭivik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atu avi tamiḻeḻuḻuttukkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭiyāta niliyil vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar kiyāṇṭa eḻuttu muṟi eṉpatiyallātu tamiḻar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟkē vantār eṉṟu uṇarttātu” 

28. tamiḻiṉ ilakkaṇak kaṭṭamippi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭum, tamiḻc coṟpiṟappu neṟi muṟikaḷip poruttiyum cintu eḻuttukkaḷi 1990 ai oṭṭip paṭikkat toṭaṅkiya irā. mativāṇaṉ muttirikaḷip patittāl kiṭikkum vācakaṅkaḷi iṭamiruntu valamākap L-> R  paṭittuḷḷār. Indus script Dravidian (1995) eṉṉum nūlil cintu veḷi nākarika muttiric coṟkaḷ 3000ttiyum perumpālāṉa toltamiḻ iyaṟ peyarkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷār. vaṇikar, talivar, kaṭavuḷar pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi aciyeḻuttukkaḷil (Morpho - syllabic) im muttirikaḷil poṟittuḷḷaṉar eṉpatum,

cāttaṉ, mācāttaṉ, cāṉaṉ, vaṅkaṉ, nattattaṉ, nantaṉ, ōmaṉ, cāmaṉ, evvi, aṉaṅkaṉ, oḷiyaṉ, cēyaṉ, atiyaṉ, avvaṉ, ayyaṉ, kō avvaṉ, yāṉaṉ, paṉṉaṉ, avuṇaṉ, kāṭṭaṉ

pōṉṟa peyarkaḷ muttirikaḷiṟ paravalāka varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum mativāṇaṉ kaṇṭu piṭippu ākum. ivar kuṟi       ai ‘ka’ eṉṟum kuṟi       ai ‘ca’ eṉṟum paṭittuḷḷār. cila muttiri vācakaṅkaḷ piṉṉar tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.     eṉṉum cuvattikam cintuveḷi eḻuttil “ōm” (ōmpuka -ōm) eṉpatu ivar kaṇṭupiṭippu. pacupati muttiriyi ivar ‘kā cā kō avvaṉ” eṉṟu paṭikkiṟār.

pukāril ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ mītu cōḻaṉiṉ puli muttiri poṟikkappaṭṭatip paṭṭiṉappāli “puli poṟittup puṟam pōkki” eṉak kuṟikkiṟatu. apporuḷ kaḷiṉ mēl vaṇikarkaḷiṉ peyar muttirikaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭati, “vampa mākkaḷ tampeyar poṟittuk kaṇṇeḻuttup paṭutta eṇṇup pal poti”eṉa cilappatikāram (5:111-112) kuṟikkiṟatu. icceytikaḷ cintuveḷi eḻuttu vācakaṅkaḷi iyaṟpeyarāka mativāṇaṉ paṭittati araṇ ceykiṉṟaṉa.




pūrṇa cantira jīvā

avaruṭiya (2004) “cintuveḷiyil muntu tamiḻ” 
nūl maṟṟum piṟṟi āyvurikaḷil kāṇum muṭivukaḷ.

29. jīvāviṉ muṭivukaḷiṉ curukkam varumāṟu:

1) cintu eḻuttu varivaṭivaṅkaḷ acinili syllabic  vaki cārntavi. (antacintuvarivaṭivaṅkaḷiliruntupiṉṉar uruvāṉa) caṅka kālat tamiḻi (tamiḻ - pirāmi) varivaṭivattiṟku aviyē mūlamākum.

ii) piṉṉar cintu varivaṭivaṅkaḷiliruntē oli nili vaṭivam phonetic script  karuttu uruvākiyatu. cintu makkaḷiṭamiruntu ik karuttik kaṭaṉvāṅkiyē (ulakiṉ mutal neṭuṅkaṇakku varici, alphabet eṉap potuvākak karutappaṭum) piṉīciya eḻuttu uruvākkappaṭṭatu. ki.mu.1500 ai oṭṭiya piṉīciya varivaṭiviliruntu pirāmiyō tamiḻiyō, uruvāyiṉaveṉpatu piḻi.

iii) nāṉūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa cintu varivaṭivaṅkaḷil oru cilavē collaciyaṉ logosyllabic  vaṭivukaḷ. ēṉiyavi iṭu kuṟivaṭivaṅkaḷ. attakiya iṭukuṟi vavaṅkaḷ 45. avaṟṟuḷ pala kūṭṭeḻuttukkaḷāka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. (tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ collamippu , puṇarcci pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ikkūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āḻnta tamiḻaṟivu uṭiyavar maṭṭumē ikkūṭṭeḻuttukaḷil aṭaṅkiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷip pirittaṟiya iyalum) cintueḻuttil 18 meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku 16 vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. mīṉvaṭivam la,ḻa,ḷa mūṉṟukkum potuvāṉatu. uyirolikaḷukku 6 vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ākamottam 22 vaṭivaṅkaḷ.

iv) cintu veḷi nakara nākarikam cumār ki.mu.1500l aḻintu viṭṭa pōtilumkirāmappuṟaṅkaḷil cintu eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ toṭarntu puḻakkattil iruntirukka vēṇṭum. kuṟippāka teṉṉāṭṭup peruṅkaṟkāla Megalithic (ki.mu.1400 - 800) kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka avi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa (kīḻavāli; perumukkal). (2006 pipravariyil cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūr kaṟkaruviyil (ki.mu. 1000-kku muntiyatu) uḷḷa cintu eḻuttukaḷ samakātēvaṉ karuttuppaṭi muru(ku) - aṉ - ? - ?”] cīvā karutti meyppikkiṉṟaṉa.

30. tamiḻi eḻuttiṉ tōṟṟatti cintuveḷi nākarika eḻuttōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttiyuḷḷatu maṭṭumiṉṟi paṇṭit tamiḻaka varalāṟṟuk kūṟukaḷ, tamiḻakat tolliyal eccaṅkaḷ pōṉvaṟṟiyum annākarikattōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttip pala ariya ceytikaḷit tantuḷḷār jīvā. eṉavē cintu nākarikam, eḻuttu paṟṟi ārvam koṇṭōr maṭṭumaṉṟi tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu ārvalarkaḷum innūlip paṭittāl avarkaḷukkup pala ariya ceytikaḷum karuttukaḷum kiṭikkum.

31. parpōlā uḷḷiṭṭa mēli aṟiñar palarum cintu eḻuttāṉatu paṭavurukkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya collaciyaṉ (Logo Syllabic) eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa uṉṉippil āyvu ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar eṉpar jīvā. cintu eḻuttu uṇmiyil kūṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiyē koṇṭatu eṉiṉum ataṉ aḻakiya ōviya - paṭa uruttaṉmi yoṉṟiyē kaṇṭu, avarkaḷ collaciyaṉ ākakkaruti iḻukkiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpati avar piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkukiṟār.

(i) ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ tolleḻuttukaḷ aṉittum paṭikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. paṭavuruttaṉmi koṇṭa cumēriya āppeḻuttu (Cuneiform), ekiptiya puṉitappoṟippu (Hieroglyphs) ‘paṭavurut taṉmi’ aṭippaṭiyil paṭikkap peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. uyirmeyyaṉ (Syllabary) eḻuttu muṟiyāṉa līṉiyar - B maṟṟum cipras eḻuttukaḷum oppīṭṭu muṟiyil paṭikkappeṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. cintu eḻuttum “muṟṟilum paṭavuruvaṉ muṟikkuriya collaciyaṉ eḻuttu muṟiyāka” iruntiruppiṉ itaṟkuḷ paṭittiruppar.

(ii) cintu eḻuttiṉip paṭikka marapumuṟip pārviyi viṭuttu (māṟṟuccintaṉi aṭippaṭiyil) evaṟṟuṭaṉum cārāta, nuṇṇāyvu tēvippaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ vaṭivam enta oru iyaṟkip poruḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyiyum koṇṭataṉṟu. avvāṟu irukakalāmeṉa mēmpōkkākak karutavikkum oṉṟiraṇṭum paṭattukkuriya poruḷic cuṭṭuvatākak karutamuṭiyavilli. nuṇuki ārāyntāl cintu eḻutti vaṭivamippu aṭippaṭiyil (1) eḷiya aṭippaṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷ, (2) kuṟiyīṭēṟṟa aṭippaṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷ, maṟṟum (3) kūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟāka pakukkalām. (ivaṟṟit tavira toṭarniliyil ēṟpaṭum iṭiveḷiyi nirappa paṭavurukkaḷ, kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, eṇkaḷ maṟṟum cintu eḻuttumuṟikku muntiya eḻuttu muṟikaḷiṉ ecca vaṭivaṅkaḷiyumkalantu ōr oruṅkiṇinta eḻuttu muṟiyi amittuḷḷaṉar.) ekiptiya eḻuttu muṟi ittakiyateṉṟu sveyṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

(iii) immūvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭiyāṉa ceyaṟkiyāṉa vaṭiva iyal (Geometric) taṉmiyuṭiya aṭippaṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷ varumāṟu



ivaṟṟi ōviyat taṉmi koṇṭa paṭavurukkaḷāka evarum karuta iyalātu. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷippaṟṟi nuṇuki ārāyvatē mutaṟpaṭiyākum.

mēṟkaṇṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉ mēlum cila eḷiyaṉavum kuṟiyīṭu ēṟkātaṉavumāṉa aṭippaṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷum cintu eḻuttuppaṭṭiyalil uḷḷaṉa. ai. makātēvaṉ (1977) cintu muttirit tokuppu nūl aṭippaṭiyil avaṟṟiyum tokuttu intap paṭṭiyalik kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu niṟivu ceyyalām.

(ivi mūṉṟuvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷiliruntum piritteṭuttavi, māṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum, oppuṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum vilakkit tokuttavi; ivaṟṟit tavira vēṟu vaṭivam etiyum cintu eḻuttil kāṭṭiyalātu eṉpatu uṟuti.

icceyaṟki vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ uruvākkam evvāṟu nikaḻntatu? potuvāka nākarika vaḷarcciyōṭu moḻivaḷarcciyum, nakara nākarika vaḷarcciyōṭu eḻuttu varivaṭiva muṟi vaḷarcciyum piṇintatu ākum. cintu nākarika nakara vaḷarcci nilikku oppāṉa vaḷarcciyi ataṉ varivaṭiva muṟiyilum kāṇkiṟōm. nakaramippu, kaṭṭakkali, aḷavi muṟikaḷ, toḻilnuṭpam eṉṟu palvēṟu tuṟikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉ mūlam peṟṟa aṉupava aṟivieḻuttu varivaṭiva amippilum payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar eṉalām. akamatu hacaṉ tāṉi (Dani) 1963 / II. 1985 nūlākiya Indian Palelography - iṉ 19m pakkamum jīvā karutti ātarikkiṟatu. kuṟiyīṭu ēṟkāta aṭippaṭivaṭivaṅkaḷ 54 tāṉ eṉpar aṟiñar tāṉi.

32. cintu eḻuttiṉ mikuvaḷarcciyum paravalum iruvaki yāṉatu eṉpar jīvā. muttiri eḻuttiṉ paraval makārāṭṭira taymāpāttuṭaṉ (Daimabad) muṭikiṟatu. eḻuttut toṭarpāṉa paraval, ilaṅki yāḻppāṇamvaric ceṉṟatu. kujarāttil uḷḷa pēṭtuvāraki, tamiḻakattil potiki malikkuki, kīḻvāli, perumukkal mutaliyaṉa; ilaṅkiyilyāḻppāṇam iṅkellām inta eḻuttu muṟiyiyum, ataṉ toṭarvaḷarcci niliyiyum kāṇalām.

(i) kujarāt pēṭtuvāraki :

vvr ṇā ṇa vara y ṇ ma
= maṇyvaraṇayāvvōr

(valamiruntu) vaṭivaṅkaḷ 1, 3 cintu eḻuttu tamiḻiyākum māṟṟattik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(ii) perumukkal (tamiḻnāṭu) :

a ra c r = aracar

eḻuttukaḷ 2, 3, 4 il tamiḻi eḻuttākum māṟṟam uḷḷatu.

(iii) kīḻvāli (tamiḻnāṭu) :

ta va ca ca tava rav = 	tavaca catavarav

tava cayya cari = 	tavacayya cari

ivvaṭivaṅkaḷ vaṭṭāra vaḻakku vaṭivaṅkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.

(iv) potiki malikkuki (tamiḻnāṭu) intiyaṉ ekspiras 23.3.1985

mū ay tava ṇa ṇ taṉ iṉ tava
= mū ay tavaṇantaṉ iṉ tava

ivveḻut tukkaḷ kālattāl muntiyaviyum cintu eḻutti naṉkaṟintavar
eḻutiyaviyum ākum.

(v) yāḻppāṇam āṉikkoṭṭaṭi (ilaṅki) The Hindu  26.4.1981 intirapālā kaṭṭuri)

ma ya ṉa = mayaṉa
t vē kō = kōvētta

itu oru kuyavar muttiri. kōvēttu - vēṭkōvar = kuyavar

mēṟkaṇṭa aintum teḷivāṉa cintu eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷākavum, tamiḻiyāka māṟum teḷivāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiyākum māṟṟattil pēṭtuvāraki toṭakka niliyiyum, perumukkal iṭiniliyiyum, yāḻppāṇam muḻumiyākat tamiḻiyākiviṭṭa iṟuti niliyiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉpiṉṉar, tamiḻiyiṉ muḻuvaḷarcci ki.mu.800 - 600 kāla aḷavil nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum.

33. cintu eḻuttu tamiḻiyākum māṟṟam tamiḻakattiltāṉ naṭipeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattup paṇṭip pāṉiyōṭukaḷil kūṭa cintu eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruppatiliruntu itaṉi aṟiyalām. tamiḻakap peruṅkaṟkāla vāḻiṭaṅkaḷākiya koṭumaṇal, cāṇūr pōṉṟavaṟṟil kiṭittuḷḷa ivviruvaki eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ veṟumpāṉikkīṟalkaḷāka maṭṭum irukka muṭiyātu e-ṭu.
cāṇūr (ki.mu.1400-800: es.ār. rāv):

cāṇūr 1400-800 BC  		L < - R
ā ya var = 		āyavar.

34. cintu eḻuttum tamiḻi eḻuttum oṉṟu kalanta niliyil kiṭippatu avaṟṟiṉ uṟavu - marapu - varalāṟṟut toṭarpit teḷivākkukiṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷiṭiyē vaṭiva - olinili oppumikaḷ mikap porunti varukiṉṟaṉa. (oṉṟiraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē caṟṟu māṟupaṭṭavi) e-ṭu.



cintu ṅa,na,ṉa meykaḷ tamiḻiyil māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. mīṉ vaṭivuṭaṉ ḻa maṭṭumē poruntukiṟatu. la-ḷa vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cintu eḻuttētum poruntavilli. eṉavē, ivi piṟkālattil uruvākkappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. tamiḻiyiṉ moḻiyākka aṭippaṭiyil pārttāl cintu eḻuttil mīṉ vaṭivam la-ḻa-ḷa mūṉṟiyum olikkap payaṉpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.

ay, av vaṭivaṅkaḷ cintuvil akara eḻuttaṭippaṭiyil oṉṟu/iraṇṭu kōṭukaḷiṭṭu amikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avvolikaḷ toṭar niliyil avvāṟē ay, av eṉa olippatāl appaṭiyē iṅku payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.

(ii) cintu eḻuttuk kūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷum, cintu aṭippaṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷik koṇṭu amikkappeṟṟaviyē. ākiyāl avaṟṟip pirittaṟintu, tamiḻiyuṭaṉ piṉvarumāṟu porutti yuḷḷār jīvā:

ikkūṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ cintu - tamiḻi eḻuttumuṟikaḷiṉ vaṭiva - olinili oṟṟumiyik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cintu - tamiḻi eḻuttukaḷiṭiyē uḷḷa vaṭiva oppumi - oli oppumi aṭippaṭiyil cintu muttirip poṟippukaḷiṉ 1977 makātēvaṉ tokuppil āṅkāṅku mātirikaḷit tiraṭṭi cilavācakaṅkaḷi tamiḻiyākap paṭittuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār jīvā (tamatu 2010 cūṉ ulakattamiḻc cemmoḻi mānāṭṭu āyvuk kaṭṭuriyil). ovvoṉṟilum iraṇṭāvatu vari “tamiḻi” ākum.

kōḻi yār iru ḻ ḻā y mu ṉ
= kōḻiyār iruḷḷāyi muṉ

35. cintu eḻuttu meyyeḻutti mikutiyākak koṇṭa ceyaṟki uyirmeyyākiya - aciyeḻuttu muṟiyeṉṟu uṟutiyākiṟatu. cintu eḻuttu nilaviya kālattilēyē, ki.mu.2250 aḷavil kirīṭ tīvil miṉōvaṉ araciṉ līṉiyar-B  uyirmeyyaṉ (Syllabary) eḻuttumuṟi vaḻaṅkiyatu. uyirmeyyaṉ eḻuttē olinili eḻuttiṉ toṭakkamākum. itiliruntē meyyeḻuttu muṟikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. cintu eḻuttu muṟiyilum paḻiya uyirmeyyaṉ eḻuttu muṟikkuriya cāṉṟeccaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. uyirmeyyaṉ muṟiyiṉ payaṉpāṭṭiluḷḷa kaṭuñ cumiyāl makkaḷ meyyeḻuttu varivaṭivaṅkaḷik uruvākkip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cintu makkaḷum ki.mu.2500 aḷavil ataṉiyē ceytirukka vēṇṭum. meyyeḻuttu muṟiyē olinili (Phonetic) eḻuttiṉ tāyākiyāl, cintu eḻuttē ulaka aḷavil olinili eḻuttiṉ tāyeṉalām.

cintu eḻuttip paṭittaṟiya utavum “rōcaṭṭā poṟippu” ākat tamiḻik kalveṭṭu eḻuttukaḷik koṇṭu, mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu cintup poṟippukaḷit tamiḻi eḻuttukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil paṭittu iraṇṭuvaki eḻuttukaḷum vaṭivanili - olinilikaḷil poruntiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu jīvā koṇṭuḷḷār.

36. jīvā āyviṉpaṭi cintu, tamiḻi, tamiḻ eḻuttu varivaṭiva oppumippaṭṭiyal varumāṟu (82%  aḷavukku oppumi uḷḷatu. cintu eḻuttukaḷ nēr kōṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ; āṉāl paṉi oliyil eḻutap perumaḷavukku potumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya tāla tamiḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaḷinta urukkaḷi aṭintaṉa)




37. “tamiḻi” aciyeḻuttu (syllabic) varivaṭivamtāṉ; eṉiṉum meyyeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷil olimāṟṟak kuṟikaḷic diacritic marks cērttu tamiḻi uruvākiyatu. ki.mu.800kkup piṉṉar “teṉṉāṭṭup peruṅkaṟkālat tamiḻa nākarittiṉariṭam tamiḻi mēlum māṟṟam aṭintatu. cintu eḻuttukaḷiṭamiruntu nēraṭiyāka olinili vaṭivi phonetic concept, piṉicīyar kaṭaṉ vāṅki piṉīciya varivaṭivi amittuk koṇṭaṉar; āṉāl olimāṟṟak kuṟikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttāmal “mey + uyir = uyirmey” eṉṟavāṟu ‘aciyeḻutti syllabary amittuk koṇṭaṉar. cintu mey eḻuttu varivaṭivaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum avaṟkaḷ tamatu piṉīciya varivaṭivattil ēṟṟup payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. iviyellām jīvāviṉ āyvu muṭivukaḷ.

cintu muttirikaḷip patittapiṉ kiṭikkum vācakaṅkaḷi eppaṭip paṭikka vēṇṭum? (perumpālōr paṭippatu valamiruntu iṭamāka (L <- R)

38. muttirikaḷip patittapiṉ kiṭṭum vācakaṅkaḷi valamiruntu iṭamākap paṭippōrē perumpāṉmiyarāvar (kāṭ Gadd,  smit, mārṣal, haṇṭar, hīrās, parpōlā, cālek, makātēvaṉ, jīvā uṭpaṭa), avarkaḷ tarum kāraṇaṅkaḷ curukkamāka:

(1) kalipaṅkaṉ ōṭṭup poṟippil (kāṇum) eṉpatil tāṉ iṟuti eḻuttu, (lāl : 1966) cintu muttirip poṟipput toṭarkaḷil U iṟuti eḻuttu; / ivviraṇṭum toṭakka eḻuttukaḷ.

(2) muttiri vācakaṅkaḷil uḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷ perumpālum iṭappakkam nōkkiyuḷḷaṉa.

(3) (a) hārappā 103 vācakattip piṉvarumāṟu paṭikka vēṇṭum:
 
(ā) moheñcotarō 735 vācakattil kaṭicik kuṟi iṭappakkattil kaṭiciyāka iṭukkil variyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.



mo 66


iṇi eṅkum kiṭṭavilli

varici 76 taṭavi varukiṟatu.

(4) iṭamiruntu valamākap paṭippatē cari eṉpataṟku rāmaṉ (1986); mativāṇaṉ (2002) ākiyōr tarum kāraṇaṅkaḷ:-

i) kalipaṅkaṉ pāṉi ōṭṭuc cil muḻumiyaṟṟatu; pāṉiyil eppaṭiyiruntatu, vācakattil entappakuti intac cillil vantatu eṉpatu teriyātu.

ii) ki.mu.1400 vari ulakil enta lipiyum L <- R  āka eḻutappaṭavilli. ataṟkuppiṉṉarum, vaṭa cemittik maṭṭumē appaṭi eḻutappaṭṭatu. ki.mu.2600 lēyēuruvāṉa cintulipi akkālattiya piṟa poṟippukaḷip pōlavē L -> R ākappaṭikka vēṇṭiyatakat tāṉ iruntirukkam. tiṭumeṉa evarum muṉṉatip piṉṉatāka māṟṟiyirār.

iii) iṉṟum L  <-  R āka uḷḷa mikaccila moḻikaḷākiya arapu mutaliyavaṟṟilum eṇkaḷ (avi L  <-  R  vaḻaṅkum intiyā viṭamiruntu peṟṟavi) L -> R ākavē eḻutapṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(iv) cintumoḻi tamiḻiya moḻi eṉṟu ēṟattāḻa muṭivāṉa niliyil tamiḻ ilakkaṇa moḻiyiyal aṭippaṭiyil (L -> R) uṭpaṭa) paṭippatē aṟivuṭimiyākum. appaṭippaṭittu “aṉittuc cintup poṟippukaḷiyum tām poruttamāka vācittu viṭṭatē L -> Rkku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟu” eṉpar mativāṇaṉ. ki.mu.1500kkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyāvil kāṇum cintu lipikaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa / cārnta poṟippukaḷilum L -> R ākavē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṟṟoru kāraṇam eṉpar.

(v) irāmaṉuṭiya 1986 kaṭṭuriyil muttiri vācakaṅkaḷil tām kāṇum taṉmikaḷ (muttirikaḷip patittuk kiṭikkum vācakaṅkaḷil alla) atāvatu:

iṭappakka iṟutiyil kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ciṟiyatāka iruppatu, allatu iṭittuk koṇṭiruppatu. (colliṉ mutal eḻutti muttiriyiṉ valappakkattiliruntu poṟikkat toṭaṅki muṭikkum niliyil iṭappakkattil iṭakkuṟivu ēṟpaṭṭatāl innilimi; iṭappakka iṟutiyil mārjiṉ illātatu; iṭappakka iṟutiyil ōriru eḻuttukaḷ oṉṟaṉ kīḻ oṉṟāka varutal; mutaliyavi

patippitta vācakaṅkaḷ L -> R āka irukkum vakiyil muttirikaḷil L -> R ākac cetukkappaṭṭati niṟuvukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa vātattik kāṇalām.

cintu eḻuttu vari vaṭivattukkum piṟṟit tamiḻi
vari vaṭivattukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu

39. cintu varivaṭivattiliruntu tamiḻi (pirami) varivaṭivu uruvākiyatu eṉṟu karutuvatu tavaṟu eṉṟa parpōlā karutti mēlē kuṟittōm. māṟāka avvāṟu tāṉ uruvākiyatu eṉpataṟkāṉa piṟa aṟiñar cilar karuttukaḷi ippirivil kāṇpōm.

40. mikap paḻiya caṅka nūlkaḷililēyē (tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷil toṉmi cāṉṟavi) tamiḻ varivaṭivam, eḻuttumuṟi kuṟittum paṇpaṭṭa ilakkiyam kuṟittum teḷivāka, virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ai. makātēvaṉ (2003)karuttu, “acōkaṉuṭiya tamiḻ – pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tamiḻ varivaṭivam uruvākkappaṭṭatu” eṉpatākum. ikkaruttu ēṟkattakkatāka illi. kāraṇam mēṟcoṉṉa ki.mu. 300 cārnta tamiḻ nūlkaḷ, pāṭalakaḷ uruppeṟuvataṟku cila pala nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ varivaṭivamum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. koṟkiyil 1970 il akaḻnteṭutta pāṉiōṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttiṉ kālam ki.mu.700 eṉa appoḻutē uṟutiyākkap paṭṭatu. koṭumaṇalil aṇmiyil akaḻnta pāṉi ōṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālamum ki.mu.500kku muntiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉa irājaṉ (2004) niṟuviyuḷḷār. paḻaṉi arukē poruntal ūril kiṭṭiya poṟippukaḷ iraṇṭiṉ kālam ki.mu. 490 / 450 eṉa 2011 iṟutiyil nirṇayikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṇa (The Hindu  15-10-11). āticcanallūril 2005il naṭanta akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭa pāṉi ōṭṭup poṟippiṉ kālamum karimak kaṇippiṉpaṭi ki.mu.700 eṉavum ceyti vantatu. ivaṟṟiyum piṟa ātāraṅkaḷiyum cuṭṭi irājaṉ (2004), naṭaṉakācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr “tamiḻukku ki.mu.800ai oṭṭiyē oru taṉi vari vaṭivam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. ataṉippiṉpaṟṟiyē acōka tamiḻ - pirāmi varivaṭivam (lipi) ki.mu. 300 il uruvākkappaṭṭirukkum” eṉpataṟkāṉa valuvāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷi muṉvittuḷḷaṉar. cintu nākarika lipi tirāviṭa moḻi cārntatu āṉapaṭiyāl pirāmi lipiyum atiliruntē uruvākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar hīrās New Review;  cūli (1936).

41. intiya moḻikaḷil tamiḻ oṉṟil tāṉ eḻuttukkaḷ neṭuṅkaṇakku niliyi aṭivataṟku muṉṉiya kālaṅkaḷil peṟṟirunta (cittira eḻuttu, uru eḻuttu, uṇarvu eḻuttu pōṉṟa) uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. icceytikaḷi yellām virivāka ti.nā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉ taṉatu ‘paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ’ (1938) nūlil tantuḷḷatuṭaṉ, intiya eḻuttu muṟikaḷ ellām tamiḻ eḻuttiliruntē tōṉṟiyirukkalām eṉpar.

“pirāmi lipi mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟpaṭavilli yeṉṟum uyir eḻuttukkaḷuḷ a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappiyaḷippatum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ varkka eḻuttukkaḷik koḷḷātatumāṉa oru pāṣikkeṉa amikkap paṭṭup piṉṉāl vaṭamoḻikku upayōkappaṭum paṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum eṇṇavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. intiyāvil uḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ ivvitam uḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ pirāmi mutalil tamipakkeṉa amintalipiyāka iruttal kūṭumā? iḵtu ārāytaṟkuriyatu”.

vaṭanāṭṭu varalāṟṟiñar es. ār. kōyal (1979) karuttum acōka pirāmi eḻuttu paṭippaṭiyāka (vaṭanāṭṭil) uruvāṉatalla; atu oru kālakaṭṭattil (ēṟattāḻa ki.mu.300
eṉakkūṟalām) putitāka uruvākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. 

yāḻppāṇam āṉik kōṭṭiyil 1980l intirapālā kaṇṭeṭutta muttirit takaṭṭil piṉvarumāṟu “tīvukō” eṉṉum col cintu eḻuttilum, piṟkālat tamiḻ eḻuttilum amintuḷḷatu eṉpar mativāṇaṉ:-

tī 	vu 	kō

cintuveḷi eḻuttu
tamiḻi

itiliruntu cintu veḷi aciyeḻuttukkaḷ (Syllabic) tol tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷāka intiyāveṅkum (īḻattilum) vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum piṉ iṭi / kaṭic caṅkat tamiḻp pulavar avaṟṟit tirutti “mutaleḻuttu” eṉṉum peyaril neṭuṅkaṇakkāka (Alphabetic) putiya eḻuttu muṟiyi ki.mu.1800-1700 ai oṭṭi uruvākkiyirukka vēṇṭum; atuvē piṟkāla “tamiḻi”; atiliruntu vaṭa intiyarkaḷ “pirāmi” eṉṟa peyaril tam moḻikku eḻuttukaḷi ākkik koṇṭaṉar eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ karuttu. itaṉ virivi avaruṭiya “Indus script among Dravidian Speakers”  (1995) nūlil kāṇka. kīḻavāli mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa pāṟi ōviya eḻuttukaḷ pōṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya palvēṟu tol eḻuttukaḷum cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi eṉpati annūlil viḷakkukiṟār.

42. ṭākṭar kē.vi. ramēṣ eḻuti ICHR (teṉ maṇṭalam peṅkaḷūr) 2006il veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa “Indian inscriptions - a study in comparison and contrast”  eṉṉum nūl teḷivāka tamiḻi (tamiḻ - pirāmi) lipi kaṇṭippāka acōkaṉukku muntiyatu; anta lipiyic caṟṟē māṟṟi acōkaṉ kalveṭṭu lipi uruvākkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu valuvāṉa ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkukiṟatu. annūliṉ (pak 7-8) muṭivu varumāṟu:

(1) acōkaṉ kālattukku vekukālattukku muṉṉarē teṉṉintiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil paḻantamiḻiṉ oliyaṉkaḷukku phonemes arumiyākap poruntum tamiḻi lipi uruvākip puḻakkattil iruntatu. (kaṟārāka evvaḷavu kaḻipaḻaṅkālattil iruntu anta lipi vaḻaṅki vantatu eṉpati niṟuva ituvaric cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭavilli).

(2) vaṭa ilaṅrika aṉurātapuram akaḻvil kaṇṭa (ki.mu. 5-4 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cārnta pāṉiōṭu oṉṟil (intō - āriya varkka eḻuttu mutaliya oliyaṉkaḷ etuvum varāta) aramā eṉṟa puttamataccol tamiḻi lipiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. oricā - āntirak karikkum vaṭa ilaṅkikkum iṭiyil akkālattil (ēṉ ataṟku neṭuṅkālam muṉaṉar iruntē) vaṇikam ceytu vanta kappal vaṇikar vacittu vanta iṭattil tāṉ avvakaḻvu naṭantatu. [camaṇar pannavana cutta kuṟippiṭum 18 paḻiya lipikaḷil tamiḻi (Damili: piṉṉar itu Dramila  - >  Dravida eṉṟu urumāṟiyatu) polinti Polindi ākiyaviyum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiyic caṟṟē māṟṟi akkappal vaṇikar uruvākkik koṇṭatē polinti].

(3) antak kappal vaṇikarkaḷ tām tamiḻi (polinti) lipiyi mutaṟkaṇ teluṅkuppakutiyil paṭṭiprōluvukkum piṉṉar vaṅkāḷam, iṉṟiya uttarap piratēcattiṉ kīḻippakuti, rājastāṉ eṉṟavāṟu paṭippaṭiyākap parappiṉar. avvāṟu parappumpoḻutu pirākirutam, camaskirutam ākiyavaṟṟukkut tēvippaṭṭa varkka eḻuttukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟukkāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum paṭippaṭiyāka uruvākkik koṇṭaṉar.

(4) mēṟcoṉṉa tamiḻi - polinti lipiyit tamatu poṟippukaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttiya `acōkaṉ āṇik kalveṭṭup poṟippāḷarkaḷ’ -- avvācakaṅkaḷi uruvākki, eḻutic cetukkiyavarkaḷ -- vaṭa intiya (pirākiruta - camskiruta) moḻikaḷukkut tēviyāṉa pala mukkiyamāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷit tamiḻi lipiyil ceytu koṇṭaṉar. āṅkila mūlam varumāṟu:

1. There was the Damili script, fully answering to the phonetic needs of ancient Tamil in use in the Pandyan tract of the extreme south of the peninsula even before the days of Asoka, though we have, at present, no means of asserting how much before.

2. The script is found used, without the need for using any Indo – Aryan phonetic symbols, not found in ancient Tamil, for writing out a three - letter word arama, of Buddhist import on a potsherd found at the 5th - 4th century B.C. levels, of an excavated site in northern Sri Lanka, which sherd should have belonged to the boat people who were plying between the eastern coastline of North India and northern Sri Lanka.

3. Those very boat people carried this Damili (Polindi) script first to Bhattiprolu and then to Bengal, eastern UP and Rajasthan, all the time displaying gradual transformations as demanded by Prakrit and Sanskrit phonetics.

4. It is this script that the composers, writers and engravers of the Asokan edicts used in their writings, introducing in that process, many far – reaching changes and developments which we find in the North Indian vernacular script. 

43. tamiḻnāṭṭil irunta ki.mu.1900 - ki.mu. 300 cārnta peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikattiṉ toṭarcciyē caṅka ilakkiya kālam; annākarikak kūṟukaḷiṟ perumpālāṉavi caṅka ilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum rājaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷuṭiya aṭutta muṭivākum. paltuṟi āyvukaḷ mēlum vaḷarum poḻutu
tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300kkum cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭatu eṉa aṉivarum ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppuḷḷatu.

44. ittaruṇattil iṉṉoṉṟiyum kūṟa vēṇṭum. ki.mu.300kku muṟpaṭṭa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eviyumē namakkuk kiṭikkavilli. ki.mu.300 - ki.pi. 300 kālakaṭṭattic cārnta caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum muḻumiyāka namakkuk kiṭikkavilli.

ki.pi.300ai oṭṭic caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷit tokuttavarkaḷ evvevaṟṟi viṭṭu vikkalām eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷō avaṟṟi maṭṭum tokuttuviṭṭu maṟṟavaṟṟi nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. tokuttavaṟṟilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytirukkalām. nuṇṇaṟi vuṭiyavarkaḷ itupaṟṟi 1871-liruntu terivittuḷḷavaṟṟik kāṇpōm:-

cārlas ī kōvar: teṉṉintiya nāṭṭār pāṭalkaḷ The Folk songs of Southern India) 1871: tiṭṭamiṭṭuc citikkappaṭāta allatu māṟṟiyeḻutappaṭāta tolpaḻaṅkālat tamiḻ nūl etiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. tamiḻ makkaḷ - tirāviṭa (paḻantamiḻ) ilakkiyattik kiviṭṭu purāṇakkatikaḷi nampiṉāltāṉ taṅkaḷukkup piḻippu uṇṭu eṉṟu uṇarntavarkaḷē ivvāṟu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattic citittavarkaḷ āvar. (taṉit) tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷ pala matippiḻantu oḻiyumpaṭi avarkaḷ ceytaṉar. aṟavē oḻikka muṭiyāta nērvukaḷilum annūlkaḷi ayōkkiyat taṉamākac citittu urumāṟṟi ulavaviṭṭu mūla nūlkaḷiṉ karutti uṇara muṭiyātavāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. civattampi (1986)yum ikkaruttiṉarē.

hayūnevil:ti tapropēṉiyaṉ (The Taprobanian) cūṉ 1896 “maturit tamiḻc caṅkamum ataṉ nūlakamum aḻikkap paṭṭapoḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cakaṭṭumēṉikku aḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. (avaṟṟuḷ camaṇak koḷki vāṭi irukkalām eṉṟa karuttil). īḻattilmakāvamcam iruntatupōl pāṇṭiyarkaḷ varalāṟṟik kūṟum oru nūl maturic caṅka nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka iruntaaḻikkappaṭṭu ataṟkup patilāka tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭu irukka vēṇṭum. aḻikkap paṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūliṉ citivukaḷuṭaṉ, aiṅkamarkaḷ (pārppaṉiya civa matattavarkaḷ) eṇṇam, camayamākiyavaṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa puṉi katikaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉip piṇintu appurāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭatu.”

jāṉ spiyars “ñāṉam tōṉṟiya iṭattiliruntu” vālyūs Values II - 10 cūli 1957 : “cintuveḷinākarika muttirikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa (toltamiḻ) vācakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭal koṇṭa (aḻintupōṉa) paṇṭi nūlkaḷip paṟṟit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum puttar kālamākiya ki.mu.600kku muntiya nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟukūṭa kiṭikkātataṟkāṉa kāraṇatti nām uṇara muṭikiṟatu. (tol tamiḻaruṭiya) paṇṭiya āvaṇaṅkaḷi āriyarkaḷ oṉṟu viṭāmal aḻittuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatē atu”


cintu muttiri varivaṭivaṅkaḷi intō-āriya (allatu vēṟu iṉa) moḻiyākap paṭittuḷḷōr muyaṟcikaḷum; avi eḻuttu, coṟkaḷik kāṭṭum “lipiyē alla” eṉṟu kūṟum cilaruṭiya niliyum.

45. cintu muttiri eḻuttukaḷi intō āriya moḻiyākavō, vēṟu iṉa moḻiyākavō, ōviyap pāṭaṅ kaṟpikka utavum karuvikaḷ pōṉṟaviyākavō karutum vinōtamāṉa uṉṉippukkaḷ varumāṟu:

1) cuvāmi caṅkarāṉantā (1943): camskiruta tantira nūl vācakaṅkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷār.

2) pi.em.pāruvā : tantira vācakaṅkaḷē muttirikaḷil uḷḷaṉa.

3) ṭi.eṉ.rāmaccantiraṉ (1956) : vēḷvikaḷil tarum taṭciṇikaḷukkāṉa ṭōkkaṉkaḷ tāṉ immuttirikaḷ vēḷvik kāṭcikaḷē muttirikaḷil uḷḷaṉa.

4) ār.em.nāt (ṣillāṅ) 1959 : muttirikaḷ tāyattu entiraṅkaḷ tām.

5) es.kē.rē : paḷḷikaḷil ilakkaṇam kaṟṟuk koṭukkap payaṉpaṭṭa villikaḷ tām ivi.

6) ālaṉ es.ci.rās (1937) : “mokañcotarō eḻuttiṉ eṇ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ” (The Numeral signs of the Mohenjadaro script)) eṉṉum taṉatu nūlil ivi toṉmiyāṉa intōṉēciya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟār.

7) ṭākṭar eṉ jā (1995) : “cintu muttirikaḷil vētac coṟkaḷ” (Vedic glossary on indus seals) 

8) ē.cuvāmi nātaṉ (1995) : “kalik kaṇṇōkkil cintu veḷik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ”. ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ eḻuttukkaḷē alla. ōviyam, naṭaṉam, ciṟpam ākiyavaṟṟi māṇavarkaḷukku kaṟpikka utaviyāka uruvākkiyavi eṉkiṟār.

9) pi.vi.cupparāyappā (1996) : “cintu veḷi eḻuttu iyalpum amippum” “Indus script, its nature and structure” - ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ vēḷāṇ poruḷ uṟpatti, payaṉpāṭṭu vivaraṅkaḷik kāṭṭum kaṇakku villikaḷ.

10) es.ār.rāv : ivar 1982 lum 1991lum veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷil cintu veḷi muttirikaḷil camskiruta vācakaṅkaḷākap paṭittuḷḷa cila varumāṟu. 

bhag - rk  pāka rk (pāka arkka = tēva arkka) 
bag - bhag - rk - a – ha  pak pāk rk aha (peruntēvaṉ arkka - viṉ muttiri) 
rama trida osa  rāma tirita ōca (virumpat takkatu, mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷil oḷi viṭuvatu)

11) ekparṭ rikṭar - aṣāṉs (Egbert Richter - Ushans) 1997 veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa The Indus script and the Rg Veda āriyar ki.mu.1700-ai oṭṭi nuḻinta piṉṉar, āriyarallāta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷtām vētakāla camskiruta moḻikku uruk koṭuttaṉar; āriyarallāta (cintu veḷi makkaḷic cārnta) riṣikaḷum piṟarum rikvēta slōkaṅkaḷi eḻutiṉar; avaṟṟil cila pala slōkaṅkaḷ cintuveḷi muttirikaḷilum poṟikkap paṭṭavi; eṉṟavāṟu ivar ūkam celkiṟatu. inta aṭippaṭiyil ivar pala muttirikaḷil rikvēta slōkaṅkaḷip paṭikkiṟār.

12) eṉ.jā maṟṟum eṉ.es.(2000) The Deciphered Indus Script - Methodology, Readings, Interpretations  ivarkaḷ cintup poṟippukaḷi vēta kāla camaskirutamākap paṭittuḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷ muṭivukaḷ tavaṟu eṉpati viḷakkum mikkēl viṭcel, sṭīv pārmar, askō parpōlā, ai.makātēvaṉ ākiyōr kaṭṭurikaḷi Frontline  (ceṉṉi) 13-10-2000, 24.11.2000 itaḻkaḷil kāṇka.

46. vaṭa intiya āyvāḷar palar cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyar varukikku muntiyatu eṉpati ēṟka maṟuttu annākarikamum vētakāla nākarikamum oṉṟē eṉṟu vallaṭi vaḻakkāka muṭivu ceytu muttiri eḻuttukaḷi eppaṭiyāvatu vētakāla camskirutamākap paṭikka muyalvati parpōlāvum (1994) kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. rāv paṭittuḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷippaṟṟi kamil svelapil nakiccuviyuṭaṉ kūṟiyatu (1985):

“cintu veḷi muttiric ciṉṉaṅkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavi colleḻuttukaḷ. āṉāl es, ār.rāv avaṟṟi eppaṭiyāvatu camskirutam ākap paṭittāka vēṇṭum eṉak karuti (uṭpiṇippu nili moḻiyākiya) camskirutatti oru vinōtamāṉa ‘ōracic col moḻiyāka’ urumāṟṟam ceytuḷḷār. rāv paṭittavaṟṟic cariyeṉṟu oppuk koḷḷa mūṭappaṟṟu maṭṭum iruntāl pōtātu. ōraḷavu mūḷik kōḷāṟum uḷḷavarkaḷālēyē itic cariyeṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyum”

47. iṟutiyāka oṉṟu. 2004l Steve Farmer, Richard Sproat, Michel witzl  ākiya mūvarum cērntu eḻutiya oru kaṭṭuriyil ‘cintu nākarika muttirikaḷil uḷḷavi eḻuttukaḷē aṉṟu’ eṉṟa vintik karuttu oṉṟit terivittaṉar. (The collapse of the Indus Script thesis. The Myth of a literate Harapan Civilisation  eṉpatu akkaṭṭuriyiṉ talippu. veḷivantatu Electronic Journal of Vedic Studies  11-2-2004 pak 1957). antak kaṭṭuriyi maṟuttu vantuḷḷa āṇittaramāṉa maṟuppukaḷ varumāṟu:

1) askō parpōlā: cūli 2007 amerikka stāṉ pōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattil uri. 16 - pepravari 2008 atē uriyi ceṉṉiyil rōcāmuttiyā nūlakak kūṭṭattil mīṇṭum nikaḻttiṉār; (ai.makātēvaṉ pārāṭṭu malar AIRAVATI  2008 il avvuri uḷḷatu).

2) mācimō vitāl Massimo Vidale  ittāliyiliruntu veḷivarum EAST AND WEST (2007) itaḻiṉ pakkaṅkaḷ 333-366l The collapse melts down – a reply to Farmer, Sproat and Witz  eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyil aṅkataccuvipaṭa arumiyāka maṟuttu ammūvariyum kiṇṭal ceytuḷḷār.

3) ai.makātēvaṉum (mēlum cila āyvāḷarkaḷum): amerikka SCIENCE (24.4.2009) itaḻil ammūvar muṭivukaḷi virivāka aṟiviyal pūrvamāka maṟuttuḷḷaṉar. akkaṭṭuriyiṉ curukkatti ai.makātēvaṉ THE HINDU  3-5-2009- il tantār; avaruṭiya piṉvarum karuttu muṟṟilum ēṟkattakkatākum.

“curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, cintu veḷippakutiyil (aṉṟu) vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyiṉ (atu tamiḻiya moḻiyākattāṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum) vācakaṅkaḷtām annākarika muttirikaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṟuvuvataṟku acikka muṭiyāta tolliyal maṟṟum moḻiyiyal cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpatē eṉatu karuttu ākum.”

`eḻuttē aṉṟu’ eṉpōrit teruṭṭutal ollātu; ākaliṉ avarkaḷi maṟuttuk koṇṭiruttal vīṇ vēliyām.


iyal 10
varalāṟṟukkuc caṟṟumuntiya (Protohistoric) kālat
tamiḻaka varalāṟu (ki.mu.1500 - ki.mu.300)

ēṟattāḻa intiyā muḻuvatumē tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) nākarikamāka irunta varalāṟṟukku muntiya Prehistoric / Protohistoric  kālamāṉa ki.mu.7000 - 1800 paṟṟi muṉ iyalkaḷil kaṇṭōm. avi perumpālum cintu maṟṟum vaṭamēṟku intiya kujarāt pakutikaḷic maṭṭumē vivarittaṉa. (ēṉiya pakutikaḷilum āṅkāṅku -- kuṟippāka kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷil -- cāṉṟukaḷ iṉi akaḻāyvu mutaliyavaṟṟil kiṭikka vāyppu uṇṭu) tamiḻakattip poṟuttavari (ki.pi.300 varit “tamiḻakam” eṉpatu vēṅkaṭattiṟku teṟkiluḷḷa teṉṉāṭu muḻuvatiyum koṇṭatu).

2. muṉpattiyil kuṟitta parappilaminta tamiḻakattiṉ purōṭōhisṭari kālavaralāṟu ivviyalil curukkamāka tarappaṭukiṟatu. kāraṇam cintu nākarikamum ituvum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpuṭiyavi. [tirāviṭar ēṟṟattil Dravidian Ascent ki.mu.3000 ai oṭṭi cintup pakutiyil nākarikam ciṟappuṟa viḷaṅkiya kālattavi eṉṟu uṟutiyākat tolliyalāḷar ēṟkum cāṉṟukaḷ teṉṉintiyāvilum kiṭṭiṉāl (avi kumarikkut teṟkilum piṟa pakutikaḷilumuḷḷa (Continental Shift) kaṇṭattiṭṭup pakutiyilum kiṭṭalām) cintu nākarikak kālattilēyē atu pōṉṟa ciṟappuṟṟa nākarikam tamiḻnāṭṭilum viḷaṅkiyatu eṉa niṟuvak kūṭiya kālamum virivil vara vāyppu uḷḷatu]. itupaṟṟiya cila varumāṟu.

3. tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil varalāṟṟuk kālat toṭakkam eṉa potuvāka iṉṟu ēṟkappaṭum caṅka kālam ki.mu.300 - ki.pi.300 cārntatu. ataṟku muntiya “varalāṟṟukku muntiya kālam” ākiya ki.mu.1000-300 paṟṟiya ceytikaḷit tiṟampaṭat taruvatu rājaṉ (2004) tolliyal nōkkil caṅkakālam ākum.  āticcanallūril uḷḷa ki.mu.1000 kālattiya iṭukāṭu 114 ēkkar parappuḷḷatu. atil 1 viḻukkāṭu maṭṭumē akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. koṭumaṇalil (caṅkakālak koṭu maṇam) 50 ēkkar parappuḷḷa paḻiya vāḻviṭattilum 1 viḻukkāṭu maṭṭumē akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta oru viḻukkāṭu akaḻvāyvukaḷil kiṭitta eccaṅkaḷiliruntē ki.mu.1000 mutal tamiḻakattil vāḻnta makkaḷē, iṭiyīṭiṉṟi, caṅka ilakkiyam vivarikkum tamiḻar camutāya makkaḷāyiṉar eṉṟu niṟuva iyaṉṟuḷḷatu. makātēvaṉ (2003) Early Tamil Epigraphy nūlil tamiḻik kalveṭṭukaḷ, poṟippukaḷ evaṟṟiyum ki.mu.200kku muntiyatākak karutavilli. āṉāl avaṟṟil pala iṉṟu ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu ataṟkum muntiyavi eṉa niṟuvappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati mēlē
kaṇṭōm.

4. ki.mu.1000 - ki.mu.300 kālakaṭṭamē tamiḻakattiṉ irumpukkālam allatu peruṅkaṟpaṭic ciṉṉak (Megalithic) kālam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. akkāla kaṭṭattil iṟantavarkaḷ tāḻiyil putikkappaṭṭaṉar. kaṟpatukkikaḷil aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. peruṅkaṟpaṭic ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cilaviṭaṅkaḷil iruntaṉa; cila viṭaṅkaḷil illi; eṉiṉum ikkālam “peruṅkaṟpaṭic ciṉṉakkālam’ eṉṟē potuvāka kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.

5. ki.mu.1000 aḷavil makkaḷ perumaḷavukku āṭumāṭu vaḷarppavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. vēḷāṇmi toṭakka niliyil iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu akaḻvāyvukaḷiṉ muṭivākum. koṭumaṇal, tāṇṭikkuṭi pāṉiōṭṭut tamiḻiṉ poṟippuk kālam ki.mu.5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉa rājaṉ niṟuviyuḷḷār. paḻaṉi arukē ‘poruntal’ eṉṉum ūril kiṭṭiya iraṇṭu poṟippukaḷ ki.mu. 490, 450 kālattavi eṉa niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (The Hindu  5-10-2011) appoḻutē eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟa camutāyam tamiḻakattil iruntatu. eṉavē varalāṟṟuk kālamē tamiḻakattil ki.mu.500liruntu toṭaṅkiyatākak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟa rājaṉ karuttu valuvāṉatākum.

6. tamiḻiya (Dravida) moḻi pēcunar ki.mu.2000kku muṉṉar iruntē tamiḻakattilum teṉṉāṭṭilum iṭiviṭātu vacittu vanta maṇṇiṉ mintarē eṉpatiyē tolliyal āyvukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati ālciṉ tampatiyar 1988 tam nūlil terivippati iyal 1il kaṇṭōm.

7. cintuveḷi muttiri eḻuttukaḷiliruntē caṅkakālat tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ uruvāyiṉa eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ, jīvā pōṉṟōr karuttu. [parpōlā pōṉṟōr iti ēṟpatilli.] ēṟattāḻa ki.mu.700kku muṉṉamē tamiḻukku ōr eḻuttu vari vaṭivam
iruntatu; atip piṉpaṟṟiyē acōka pirāmi eḻuttu uruvākkappaṭṭatu; eṉak koḷvataṟkāṉa (kē.vi. ramēṣ (2006) uḷḷiṭṭōr tanta)ātāraṅkaḷi mēlē kaṇṭōm. 2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟi - cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi.caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putukkaṟkālakkaruvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷ nāṉkiṉi ai.makātēvaṉ “muruku aṉ” (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār. akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu.2000- 1500 āka irukkalām eṉpar. putukkaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orē moḻiyiyē pēciṉar; ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē eṉpati ippoṟippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpatu avar muṭivu ākum. (The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu and the Indus Valley people shared the same language which can only be Dravidian and not Indo- Aryan; 1.5.2006. The Hindu).
